# Playtest: mixed_resist_then_book
seed=110
interpreter=OllamaInterpreter
narrator=OllamaNarrator

## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you find yourself waiting, your breath hitching against the damp air. The room remains unresponsive, the bed, cup, book, and door all standing as silent witnesses to your solitude. The skin on your body, unaccustomed to such grime, feels noticeably dirty. Your voice, weak from disuse, sounds hollow as you try to speak. The cell, as ever, keeps its quiet, unbroken by the distant clatter of unseen machinery or the footsteps of unseen keepers. Time ticks by slowly, the seconds stretching out like shadows on the wall.

## Turn 3
> wait

The observation slit in the door swings open, revealing a figure on the other side. The figure speaks, their words a jumble of noise and repetition. But the message is clear: they want you to step back from the door. The cell remains silent and still, a stark contrast to the insistent urgings from beyond the slit. Your feet remain planted, your body tense, the air around you heavy with anticipation. The figure's gestures continue, their words echoing in the confined space. The cell remains quiet, the only sound the distant hum of machinery. The slit remains open, a narrow passage between you and the unknown.

## Turn 4
> I will not back away

In the dimly lit cell, the observation slit remains open, a narrow window to the world beyond. The staff member, a figure of practical silence, pauses their repetitive gesture, eyes fixed on you. A sense of anticipation fills the air as they turn, likely to summon reinforcements.

Your skin, already unclean from the cell's conditions, feels noticeably grittier. Your voice, thin and broken, fails to echo in the silent confinement. Yet, you stand firm, unyielding to the demand to step away from the door. The cell, your temporary fortress, remains unbroken by the outside's insistence.

## Turn 9
> look around

In the corridor, the cell door swings open, guided by unseen hands. Your arms are secured, the familiar grip of the institution asserting itself anew. The corridor mirrors the cell, lined with identical doors, each one a symbol of confinement. A bed, a cup, and a book abandoned at your former location now lie before you. The doorless slit at eye level offers a glimpse of the world beyond, yet it remains inaccessible from this side.

Your senses are met with the stale taste of air, the rough feel of unclean skin, the tightness of shoulders, and the restraint limiting movement. The institution moves at its own pace, and you are but a pawn in its game. The figure who led you here steps aside, making room for another — larger, more imposing, ready to escort you further whether you comply or resist. The corridor remains your temporary prison, the next step in the journey unknown.

In the corridor, the cell door swings open, guided by unseen hands. Your arms are secured, the familiar grip of the institution asserting itself anew. The corridor mirrors the cell, lined with identical doors, each one a symbol of confinement. A bed, a cup, and a book abandoned at your former location now lie before you. The doorless slit at eye level offers a glimpse of the world beyond, yet it remains inaccessible from this side.

Your senses are met with the stale taste of air, the rough feel of unclean skin, the tightness of shoulders, and the restraint limiting movement. The institution moves at its own pace, and you are but a pawn in its game. The figure who led you here steps aside, making room for another — larger, more imposing, ready to escort you further whether you comply or resist. The corridor remains your temporary prison, the next step in the journey unknown.

> stay awake

The basin, already filled with water, remains unyielding to your efforts to stay awake. The soap, its medicinal scent heavy in the air, sits untouched. The silence between the staff members is palpable, the tension in the air almost tactile. The younger one, eyes darting to you, seems on the verge of breaking some unspoken rule. ## Turn 16
> stop reading

The bowl, once a beacon of expectation, now sits untouched. The heat from the food wafts towards you, a stark contrast to the coldness that lingers within you. ## Turn 21
> wait

In the dim light of the cell, you find yourself still, a new day dawning. The institution's presence looms as the cell remains quiet, a stark contrast to the formal collection you're to endure today.

You attempted to keep vigil, a noble endeavor that ended with you waking later, your face pressed against the cold wall. The cell, unyielding, remains a constant reminder of your captivity. Your words, thin and broken, seem a feeble attempt to break the silence that envelops you.

Ruan, lucid yet fractured, continues to sort through memories, the weight of the institution's impending action hanging heavy in the air. The collection, though imminent, has not yet come. The cell, your temporary prison, remains your only companion in this moment.

The morning air is crisp, the institution's formality palpable as you're led from the cell into a corridor, a path lined with identical bolts mirroring your former cell door. Fear keeps your shoulders tight, words come out thin and broken, yet you remain silent, waiting for the next step in this formal retrieval.

## Turn 23
> wait

In the dimly lit corridor, the institutional pace persists as you're escorted further. The large, silent staff member on one side, the younger, tense one on the other. A third figure, Nessa, remains abrasive and sceptical.

Your heart pounds, fear constricting your chest. The air is heavy with tension, a tangible presence that seems to pulse with each step. The corridor remains unchanged, its cold, sterile walls a grim reminder of the institution's rigid control.

Suddenly, one of the staff members stops, turning to face you. A word is shown, a name. Yours? The air grows colder, your breath hitches, and words struggle to form. Your planted feet and clenched fists betray your inability to respond. The name, your name, feels like a heavy burden in this place of rules and secrets.

In the dimly lit corridor, the institutional pace persists as you're escorted further. The large, silent staff member on one side, the younger, tense one on the other. A third figure, Nessa, remains abrasive and sceptical.

Your planted feet and clenched fists betray your inability to respond. The name, your name, feels like a heavy burden in this place of rules and secrets.

## Turn 24
> wait

In the sterile, fluorescent-lit interview room, you find yourself face-to-face with Veyra, a staff member adorned with a fitted collar that appears as natural as everyday clothing. The silence is thick, broken only by the soft rustle of papers and the measured breathing of the room's occupants.

A table, adorned with pictures and objects, stands between you, reminiscent of a lesson in a school long forgotten. One of the staff members, who seems controlled and kind, begins to ask questions, their words slow and deliberate. The images depict low houses and water, a possible reference to home.

Your voice, however, falters, the words coming out thin and broken, a stark contrast to your planted feet and firm grip. Fear keeps your shoulders tight, a physical manifestation of the tension in the room. The questioning continues, the staff members waiting patiently for your answers.

In the dimly lit facility, the chilling air and the sensation of wood beneath your feet bring a sense of childhood, a memory that surfaces unprompted.
